Abstract: In order to understand which initial states retain memory under periodic driving, it is necessary to depart from the ideal limit giving rise to invariant structures and work inside near-degenerate manifolds. We show that degeneracy itself is not conclusive and what actually matters is how perturbations, such as structural and driving imperfections, acts on the projected multiplet. For systems decoupling into the local clusters in the ideal limit, the projections can be computed within degenerate perturbation theory. There the stability is decided by two criteria: the coupling can not connect the state with other degenerate partners in the manifold, either by kinetic blocking, or more generally, by diagonalizing the projected coupling, and the drive's local selection rule must admit only transfer energies detuned from Floquet sidebands, in which case the projected drive generator exactly vanishes. We formalize these understandings in a periodically driven clean, short-ranged alternating XXZ chain and demonstrate a set of results that include, long-lived states, both of product form and entangled across the clusters, a periodic family of sideband resonances, and an explicit example showing opposite fates for states with identical energy and charges.

</details>

Abstract: Validating variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) pipelines on realistic chemical systems is a critical stepping stone toward tackling classically intractable molecules. ### [82] [Coherent error threshold for quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20537)
*Zhengyi Han,Yuanchen Zhao,Yijia Xu,Yixu Wang,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一般 qLDPC 码在局部相干噪声（更一般地局部信道噪声）下具有非零码容量阈值：当距离 $d=\Omega(\log n)$ 时，存在常数噪声强度阈值，使得最优恢复与最小权重解码器的逻辑恢复误差（以 diamond 距离度量）随码距指数衰减。核心技术是"簇重求和"（cluster resummation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC 码对随机 Pauli 噪声具有非零阈值的优势已被广泛认识，但相干误差本质不同：它产生错误模式的叠加，其振幅在 syndrome 测量后仍可干涉。对相干误差的严格理解仍然有限，缺乏通用的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 提出"簇重求和"（cluster resummation）技术：不再逐个界住叠加的错误构型，而是在信道展开中分离出一个大的连通错误簇，先对与它不连通的所有错误进行精确重求和，再取范数；随后用标准簇计数论证导出指数抑制。分析同时覆盖最优恢复与最小权重解码器。

Result: 对任意满足 $d=\Omega(\log n)$ 的 qLDPC 码族，存在常数噪声强度，低于该强度时最优恢复与最小权重解码器的逻辑恢复误差在 diamond 距离下随码距指数衰减；因而一般 qLDPC 码具有非零码容量阈值对抗局部相干噪声与更一般的局部信道噪声。

Conclusion: 本文解决了容错理论中长期存在的难题，为 qLDPC 码对抗相干噪声提供了通用鲁棒性保证，并为未来容错量子技术研究奠定严格基础。

Abstract: A key appeal of quantum low-density parity check (qLDPC) codes is their ability to suppress stochastic Pauli noise below nonzero thresholds. Coherent errors are fundamentally different: they produce superpositions of error patterns whose amplitudes can interfere even after syndrome measurement. Rigorous understanding of coherent errors remains limited. Here we show that general qLDPC codes admit a nonzero code capacity threshold against local coherent noise and more generally local channel noise. For any family of qLDPC codes with distance $d=Ω(\log n)$, we show that there is a constant noise strength below which the logical recovery error in diamond distance decays exponentially with the code distance. The result is established for optimal recovery as well as the minimum-weight decoder. The key technical ingredient is what we call a \emph{cluster resummation}: rather than bounding superposed error configurations one by one, we isolate a large connected error cluster in the channel expansion and exactly resum all errors disconnected from it before taking norms. Standard cluster counting then yields exponential suppression. This work resolves a longstanding challenge in fault tolerance theory, providing general robustness guarantees for qLDPC codes against coherent noise and laying a rigorous foundation for future studies of fault-tolerant quantum technologies.

</details>


### [83] [Technical Report OFDM-Assisted Simultaneous Quantum and Classical THz Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20542)

Abstract: The feasibility of cost-effective simultaneous quantum and classical communication (SQCC) transmitting both the quantum key and classical information via a superimposed coherent state is investigated both in optical and Terahertz (THz) bands. Since the existing THz SQCC schemes assume single-carrier (SC) transmission over flat fading channels, we embark on investigating SQCC in realistic frequency-selective multipath THz fading channels. We then propose an orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) based SQCC system for time-invariant frequency-selective THz scenarios, supported by low-density parity-check coded (LDPC) multidimensional QKD reconciliation schemes. Our simulation results demonstrate that the OFDM-based SQCC scheme is capable of achieving a practical secret key rate (SKR) over a wide range of power sharing scenarios between the classical and quantum signals. By contrast its single-carrier counterpart requires the classical signal to be at least 100 times stronger than the quantum signal in THz SQCC.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用规模的量子计算需要执行长而复杂的算法，端到端错误率须远低于物理量子比特直接支持的水平。纠错后的逻辑量子比特是实现该目标所必需的。然而，为使逻辑量子比特的进展可度量、可比较，当前缺乏统一定义与评价框架。

Method: 作者提出"可扩展逻辑量子比特"（scalable logical qubits）的正式定义，其需满足三个条件：通过重复量子纠错在长计算中保持逻辑量子比特；支持带低延迟实时解码与反馈的容错通用操作；可按应用需求复制到数百或数千个。随后沿四个耦合维度——可靠性（reliability）、规模（scale）、能力（capability）、性能（performance）——对其进行刻画，并分析各维度间的权衡。

Result: 给出了可扩展逻辑量子比特的规范化定义及其四维刻画框架，明确了判断逻辑量子比特是否真正"可扩展"所需的关键属性，并揭示了四个维度之间相互耦合、存在取舍的关系。

Abstract: Utility-scale quantum computing will require executing long, complex algorithms with end-to-end error rates far below what physical qubits can support directly. Error-corrected logical qubits are needed to achieve this goal. To make the progress on logical qubits measurable and comparable we introduce the definition of scalable logical qubits: logical qubits preserved for long computations by repeated quantum error correction, capable of fault-tolerant universal operations with low-latency real-time decoding and feedback, and replicable to the hundreds or thousands, as required by applications. We characterize these scalable logical qubits along four coupled dimensions: reliability, scale, capability, and performance, and discuss trade-offs among these dimensions.

</details>


### [85] [FT-Weave: Real-Time Compilation Framework for Reconfigurable Fault-Tolerant Quantum Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20573)
*Wan-Hsuan Lin,Milan Kornjača,Chen Zhao,Sheng-Tao Wang,Jason Cong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: FT-Weave：面向早期容错量子计算的分阶段实时编译框架，联合协调资源制备、分配、路由与纠错，最高实现3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子计算需要容错量子计算（FTQC）支撑，但受严格硬件与时序约束，资源制备、分配、路由与逻辑执行难以协调。现有FTQC编译多采用离线调度，基于名义或固定魔术态工厂吞吐量，无法响应随机的资源制备与传态结果，导致执行停顿与硬件利用率不足。

Method: 提出FT-Weave，一种分阶段感知的实时FTQC编译框架，联合协调资源制备、资源分配、传态路由与纠正处理；通过适应运行时资源可用性与硬件约束，使制备、通信与逻辑执行相互重叠。在两种代表性中性原子早期FTQC架构上实例化：横向STAR与T态培养（cultivation）架构。

Result: 在评估的硬件与时延模型下，针对二维横场Ising模型的模拟，FT-Weave相比基线编译流程最高取得3倍加速。案例研究还发现：最大化暴露并发度并不必然最小化执行时间——细粒度异步执行虽能减少局部空闲，但其更小的优化窗口与更高的路由竞争可能抵消收益。

Conclusion: 有效FTQC资源转化为应用吞吐的效率取决于运行时协同调度，而非单纯暴露并行度。FT-Weave为跨资源协议与架构解决实时编排问题提供了蓝图。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) is essential for large-scale quantum computation, but realizing useful application throughput requires coordinating resource preparation, assignment, routing, and logical execution under strict hardware and timing constraints. Many FTQC compilation approaches construct offline schedules using nominal or fixed magic-state factory throughput. Such schedules cannot respond to stochastic resource-preparation and teleportation outcomes, leading to execution stalls and hardware underutilization. In this work, we introduce FT-Weave, a stage-aware real-time FTQC compilation framework that jointly coordinates resource preparation, resource assignment, teleportation routing, and correction handling. By adapting to runtime resource availability and hardware constraints, FT-Weave allows preparation, communication, and logical execution to overlap. We instantiate FT-Weave on two representative neutral-atom, early FTQC architectures: transversal STAR and a T-state cultivation architecture. Under the evaluated hardware and latency model, FT-Weave achieves a speedup of up to 3X over a baseline compilation flow for simulations of the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model. In the case study, we further find that maximizing exposed concurrency does not necessarily minimize execution time. Although fine-grained asynchronous execution can reduce local idle time, its smaller optimization windows and increased routing contention can outweigh these gains. Together, these results show that effective runtime coordination, rather then exposed parallelism alone, determines how efficiently FTQC resources translate into application throughput:FT-Weave provides a blueprint for solving this real-time orchestration problem across resource protocols and architectures.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CVQKD) is compatible with telecommunication infrastructure, but implementing composable security with experimentally practical resources has remained challenging, particularly for discrete-modulated (DM) protocols. We report the first experimental demonstration of a DM CVQKD system that generates composable secret keys against general attacks with finite-size block lengths on the order of $\sim 10^{6}$ rounds via a quadrature phase shift keying (QPSK) system. Our implementation follows a variable-length, general security framework enabled by modern entropy accumulation techniques and conic optimization, whose experimental pipeline allows real-time operation on near-commercial hardware.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor在近三十年前猜想任意量子二分（两个态构成的系综）的可访问信息由投影测量达到，但长期仅有量子比特情形被Keil证明，任意维情形仍是公开难题。本文旨在彻底解决这一长期悬而未决的问题。

Method: 一，证明Shor猜想可推广至任意维，方法是由Fang、Fawzi与Fawzi的近期结果推出该猜想，并给出自包含的初等证明；二，对任意给定二分与任意测量，构造显式投影测量，使其从该二分提取的信息量超过该测量；三，将二分可访问信息的计算表述为凸优化问题。

Result: Shor猜想在任意维度成立；对任意二分与任意测量，均可显式构造出性能更优的投影测量；二分可访问信息的计算可化为凸问题。

Conclusion: 该工作彻底解决了Shor关于量子二分可访问信息由投影测量达到的长期猜想，推广至任意维，并提供显式构造与凸计算框架。

Abstract: The accessible information of any given quantum ensemble quantifies the maximum amount of Shannon information that can be extracted from the ensemble by any quantum measurement. Almost three decades ago, Shor conjectured that the accessible information of any quantum dichotomy, that is, an ensemble of two states, is attained by a projective measurement. Recently, a proof of this conjecture restricted to the qubit case was published by Keil. Here, we conclusively settle this longstanding open problem. First, we show that Shor's conjecture follows, in arbitrary dimension, from a recent result by Fang, Fawzi and Fawzi, and we provide a self-contained, elementary proof. Second, for any given dichotomy and measurement, we provide the explicit construction of a projective measurement that outperforms such a measurement in extracting information from the given dichotomy. Third, while the computation of the accessible information is known to be non-convex in general, we frame the computation of the accessible information of dihotomies as a convex problem.

</details>

Motivation: 探讨量子力学是否允许同时具备相位保持（对所有玻色相位空间角度一致）与场算符非线性的确定性放大方案，并考察此类方案对量子信息科学是否有实用价值。

Abstract: Does quantum mechanics allow for deterministic amplification schemes that simultaneously behave uniformly for all angles of bosonic phase space (i.e. phase preserving) and are nonlinear in the underlying field operators? Would such a scheme have some utility to quantum information science? In this paper we answer both questions with a resounding yes. Such amplifiers exist, but their form is highly constrained compared to the more general set of noise limited nonlinear amplifiers previously studied in the literature. This enables us to characterize the entire class of bosonic noise-limited nonlinear phase preserving amplifiers, and identify their utility: namely, the optimal amplification of Yurke-Stoler cat states and Kerr kitten states.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 焊接树中寻找入口到出口路径不存在高效量子算法，即使找到出口顶点可以指数加速，但构建完整路径无法高效实现。

Abstract: Starting from the entrance of a welded tree, a quantum walk algorithm can find its exit vertex exponentially faster than any classical algorithm. However, it has been an open question whether any quantum algorithm is able to efficiently find a path from the entrance to the exit. We answer this by proving an exponential quantum query lower bound for finding such path. Specifically, for trees of height $n$, any quantum query algorithm requires at least $Ω(2^{n/24})$ queries in order to succeed with constant probability.
  Our proof uses the compressed permutation oracle technique in order to construct databases which track the graph information an algorithm has learned and forgotten, and show that no efficient quantum algorithm can build an entrance-to-exit path in these records.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测物质相通常依赖正确序参量，但未知相变难以确定，传统靠物理直觉与经验猜测。现有神经网络方法虽能在无先验知识下定位相变，但存在黑箱特性，只识别相而不揭示相性质，且面对真实含噪实验数据时表现不佳。

Method: TetrisCNN：一种卷积架构，具有不同形状滤波器的并行分支（形似俄罗斯方块）。直接在实验快照上学习以自旋关联子表示的稀疏、可解释隐表征。应用于多基下测量的二维Ising和XY量子模拟器实验快照。

Result: 网络不仅能检测相变与跨接（crossover），还能将隐表征与决策边界表达为由实验可测自旋关联子构成的符号公式。

Conclusion: 该框架为将可解释神经网络与量子模拟器结合、发现并理解新物质相开辟了道路。

Abstract: Detecting phases of matter in general relies on identifying the correct order parameter - a task that remains notoriously difficult for unknown transitions and traditionally is guided by physical intuition and educated guess. Neural networks have recently offered an alternative route by locating phase transitions in known models without any a priori physical knowledge. Yet these approaches remain black boxes and only identify phases without elucidating their properties. Moreover, they often struggle when confronted with realistic, noisy experimental data, which constitute the ultimate testbed for automated methods in physics. Here, we bridge these perspectives by introducing TetrisCNN, a convolutional architecture with parallel branches of differently shaped filters, reminiscent of Tetris blocks, that learns sparse, interpretable latent representations directly in terms of spin correlators. Applied to experimental snapshots of two-dimensional Ising and XY quantum simulators measured in multiple bases, the network not only detects phase transitions and crossovers but also expresses its latent representation and decision boundaries as symbolic formulas built from experimentally measurable spin correlators. This framework opens the way to integrating interpretable neural networks with quantum simulators to uncover and understand new phases of matter.

</details>


### [91] [Multinegativity and single-letter formulas for asymptotic entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20698)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠的渐近度量定义在无穷多份拷贝上，单字母公式虽能将其化简为单拷贝计算但极少存在。本文旨在为关于PPT态的渐近相对熵构建可计算且层级逼近的上界，并探讨其可加性与单字母公式，同时刻画纠缠代价层级的有限坍缩问题。

Method: 构造关于PPT态渐近相对熵的可计算上界递减层级，使每一固定层级的正则化都等于该渐近量；将构造推广到sandwiched Rényi散度；引入k-多重负态（binegative态的推广）及相应的k-多重负性，用以给出渐近相对熵与精确PPT纠缠代价的显式上界；并构造任意深度k的k-多重负态来分离纠缠代价层级的相邻层级。

Result: 在任意层级的可加性即可推出单字母公式，即使通常的单拷贝相对熵不可加；在两个包含所有Werner态的广泛多参数族上于首个非平凡层级建立了可加性；构造出在任意大深度k上k-多重负的态，分离了既有权缠代价层级的相邻层级，否证了其有限坍缩的猜想。

Conclusion: 研究给出了依赖态的单字母公式，同时揭示了通用有限层级刻画方法的局限性。

Abstract: The study of entanglement inevitably leads to the concept of regularization, where measures are evaluated on infinitely many copies of a state. Single-letter formulas try to make these asymptotic entanglement quantities accessible through a calculation on one copy of a state, but are rarely available. We introduce a decreasing hierarchy of computable upper bounds on the asymptotic relative entropy of entanglement with respect to positive-partial-transpose (PPT) states. The regularization of every fixed hierarchy level equals this asymptotic quantity. Additivity at any level therefore yields a single-letter formula, even when the usual one-copy relative entropy is nonadditive. We establish such additivity at the first nontrivial level for two broad multiparameter families, both containing all Werner states. The upper-bound construction also extends to sandwiched Renyi divergences. The hierarchy motivates $k$-multinegative states, a generalization of binegative states and the associated $k$-multinegativity, which gives explicit upper bounds on both the asymptotic relative entropy and exact PPT entanglement cost. We construct states which are $k$-multinegative at arbitrarily large depths $k$ that separate consecutive levels of the previously introduced entanglement-cost hierarchy, disproving its conjectured finite collapse. These results provide state-dependent single-letter formulas while identifying a limitation of universal finite-level characterizations.

</details>


### [92] [Proof of a positive coherent-error threshold for topological quantum codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20708)
*Shiro Tamiya,Masato Koashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了在有界逻辑量子比特数的量子LDPC码（含表面码等拓扑码）中，相干Z旋转误差存在正阈值：当旋转角低于与码规模无关的常数时，最大似然Pauli恢复使纠缠失真随码距指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错码阈值分析主要针对随机误差模型，但实际设备中存在相干误差（如不完美控制导致的非期望Z旋转），随机模型无法刻画。表面码在相干误差下的阈值行为仅有数值证据，缺乏严格证明。

Method: 将傅里叶分析与抽象聚合物模型的团簇展开相结合：傅里叶分析用于保留相干误差各振幅间的干涉效应，团簇展开用于处理聚合物模型，从而在相干误差下建立阈值定理。

Result: 证明最大似然Pauli恢复可将纠缠失真在码距上指数抑制，前因子与物理量子比特数成线性关系，只要旋转角低于一个与码规模无关的常数，即相干Z旋转误差存在正阈值。

Conclusion: 结果适用于有界逻辑量子比特数的量子LDPC码，包括表面码及其他拓扑码，扩展了量子纠错的理论基础，并给出了随机误差之外的量子纠错的统计力学描述。

Abstract: Threshold analyses of quantum error-correcting codes are well established for stochastic error models, in which errors occur randomly with given probabilities. However, errors in actual devices can also be coherent, such as unwanted $Z$ rotations due to imperfect control, which are not captured by stochastic error models. For the surface code, numerical studies have indicated threshold behavior even under coherent errors, but a rigorous proof of threshold existence is lacking. Here we prove that a positive threshold for coherent $Z$-rotation errors exists for quantum low-density parity-check codes with a bounded number of logical qubits, including the surface code and other topological codes. Specifically, we show that the maximum-likelihood Pauli recovery suppresses the entanglement infidelity exponentially in the code distance up to a prefactor linear in the number of physical qubits whenever the rotation angles lie below a constant that is independent of the code size. The proof combines Fourier analysis to retain the interference among the amplitudes of coherent errors with the cluster expansion of abstract polymer models. Our results expand the theoretical foundation of quantum error correction and offer a statistical-mechanical description of quantum error correction beyond stochastic errors.

</details>

*Malte Schade,Sophia Simon,Nathan Wiebe,Scott Keating,Cyrill Bösch,Andreas Fichtner*

<details>

Motivation: 将近期无阻尼谐振系统的量子加速推广到黏声与黏弹性介质，即含非马尔可夫耗散和时变材料性质的经典振子网络，探索现实阻尼振子网络及近似波动方程的实际量子加速可能性。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子模拟加速扩展到现实阻尼振子网络与近似波动方程，为实际量子加速提供了理论基础，同时明确了指数量子优势在局域耦合拓扑下的不可能性。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for simulating classical oscillator networks characterized by non-Markovian dissipation and time-varying material properties, extending recent speedups for undamped harmonic systems to viscoacoustic and viscoelastic media. We embed the history-dependent dynamics into a Markovian state space governed by a non-Hermitian operator by approximating memory kernels through a Prony series. We then use linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation to estimate the instantaneous kinetic and potential energy for a subset of oscillators at time $t$ within error $ε$ using a number of queries to the oscillator system that scales as $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(α_{\rm tot} t/ε)$, where $α_{\rm tot}$ is polynomial in the strength of the dissipation, spring constants, inverse masses, and sparsity of the connections in the network. We further show that this energy estimation task is in the worst-case classically hard (i.e., a corresponding decision problem is $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete), even in the presence of strong dissipation. For time-dependent materials, we show that changes in material properties appear as effective dissipation or growth in the energy representation. Additionally, we prove the infeasibility of exponential quantum advantages in locally coupled topologies through a novel form of Lieb-Robinson-like bounds that apply to differential equations. This allows our quantum algorithms to provide quartic speedups for locally coupled damped oscillator systems in three dimensions, raising the possibility of practical quantum speedups for realistically damped oscillator networks and approximated wave equations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两两无偏基刻画了两个量子测量间的完美互补性，但超越成对无偏性的高阶推广虽被提出，却基本没有非平凡的高阶构造。作者希望把k-折无偏基从秩一基测量推广到任意秩投影测量，以建立更丰富的高阶理论并给出可操作的物理意义。

Method: 引入k-折无偏测量（k-UM），将arXiv:1706.04446的k-折无偏基从秩一基测量推广到任意秩投影测量；定义代数k-UM与谱k-UM，并证明在秩一测量与三元测量（3-UM）情形下二者一致；利用Hadamard矩阵与Clifford代数构造无穷多高秩三元组；给出3-UM的广义不相容鲁棒性的精确值，并在相容阈值处构造噪声版本的显式联合测量；实现New J. Phys. 28, 064509 (2026)中引入的和方差层级的对称性约化。

Result: 证明了更高阶秩一构造与三结果3-UM的强不可能性结果；借助Hadamard矩阵与Clifford代数获得无穷多高秩三元组；对任意结果数的3-UM精确确定其广义不相容鲁棒性并构造阈值处的显式联合测量；数值表明k-UM分析给出的噪声阈值可能刻画和方差层级渐近行为，并以极高精度显示所构造的四结果3-UM是最不相容的四结果测量三元组之一。

Conclusion: 高阶无偏性在高秩投影测量层面可获得丰富的构造与精确的测量不相容性解释，k-UM框架既给出不可能性边界，也提供了接近最优不相容性的具体实例，并与和方差层级的渐近噪声阈值相呼应。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases capture perfect complementarity between two quantum measurements. Extensions beyond pairwise unbiasedness have been proposed, but essentially no non-trivial higher-order constructions are known. We introduce $k$-fold unbiased measurements ($k$-UMs), extending the $k$-fold unbiased bases notion of [arXiv:1706.04446] from rank-one basis measurements to arbitrary-rank projective measurements, and show that this higher-rank setting supports a much richer theory. We develop the notions of algebraic and spectral $k$-UMs and prove that they coincide for rank-one measurements and triples of measurements (3-UMs). We establish strong no-go results for higher-order rank-one constructions and three-outcome 3-UMs, but obtain infinitely many higher-rank triples using Hadamard matrices and Clifford algebras. We then give these structures an exact operational interpretation in terms of measurement incompatibility. For 3-UMs with any number of outcomes, we determine their generalised incompatibility robustness exactly and construct an explicit joint measurement for their noisy versions at the compatibility threshold. Finally, we implement the symmetry reduction of the sum-of-squares hierarchy recently introduced in [New J. Phys. 28, 064509 (2026)] and give numerical evidence that the noise thresholds arising from the $k$-UM analysis may characterise the asymptotic behaviour of this hierarchy. In particular, with very high precision, we numerically show that our constructed four-outcome 3-UMs are among the most incompatible triples of four-outcome measurements.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲量子计算允许低资源客户端安全地将计算外包给量子服务器，但服务器资源开销随计算规模增长，成为实现瓶颈。因此需要降低服务器成本并保持安全性与容错性。

Method: 利用泡利计算（PBC）框架构建BQC协议；协议继承PBC的容错性与量子比特虚拟化；并获得经典客户端BQC在缺乏经典可模拟性下仍可行的结论；进一步提出基于纠缠的对偶协议，用于资源态计算。

Result: 服务器规模仅取决于非Clifford门数量；协议具备容错性与量子比特虚拟化；表明即使缺少经典可模拟性，经典客户端BQC仍可实现；对偶协议显著降低执行成本。

Conclusion: 通过PBC实现更高效的BQC，降低服务器资源开销并扩展其可行性边界，同时以对偶协议进一步减少执行成本。

Abstract: Blind quantum computation (BQC) allows low-resource clients to securely delegate computations to a quantum server, but server resource costs scale with the computation size, posing a bottleneck for implementations. By leveraging Pauli-based computation (PBC), we achieve BQC with a server whose size depends only on the non-Clifford gate count. Our protocol inherits fault tolerance and qubit virtualization from PBC and reveals that classical-client BQC is possible even in the absence of classical simulability. Finally, we present an entanglement-based dual protocol that performs a resource state computation with a dramatically reduced execution cost.

</details>

*Nitay Mayo,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前 N 个谐振子模式张成的空间上，模式系数与位置空间采样之间的精确基变换是量子谐振子模拟等任务的基本构件；Gauss–Hermite 求积提供了该精确变换，但需要一种门数与宽度均近线性/多对数、且误差对任意叠加与辅助寄存器都成立的可实现量子构造。

Method: 利用 Gauss–Hermite 求积给出的模式系数与 N 个加权位置采样的精确对应关系构造该变换；核心是在自适应窗口上进行带符号平均，把均匀网格采样转换为加权 Hermite 根采样，并通过窗口宽度的变化控制放大代价，从而以 polylog 量子宽度实现 O(N polylog(N,1/ε)) 逻辑门。

Result: 该变换可由 O(N polylog(N,1/ε)) 逻辑门与 polylog 量子宽度实现；算子误差界 ε 在任意叠加态上成立，并包含所有辅助寄存器；窗口宽度调节放大开销，使门数达到近线性界。

Conclusion: Gauss–Hermite 求积对应的模式–位置采样基变换可被高效量子化，获得近线性门数与多对数宽度的构造，并提供覆盖辅助寄存器的严格算子误差界。

Abstract: On the span of the first $N$ oscillator modes, Gauss--Hermite quadrature gives an exact change of basis between mode coefficients and $N$ weighted position space samples. The operator-error bound $\varepsilon$ holds on arbitrary superpositions and includes all auxiliary registers. The construction uses signed averages on adaptive windows to convert uniform-grid samples into weighted Hermite-root samples. Their varying widths control the amplification cost, giving the near-linear bound.

</details>


### [97] [Mermin-Peres magic rectangles modulo odd primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20746)
*Josse van Dobben de Bruyn,Remy van Dobben de Bruyn,Peter Zeman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对任意整数 $d\ge2$ 构造了 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 上的线性方程组，使其无非经典解但有有限维算子解，推广了 Mermin-Peres 魔方；当 $p$ 为奇素数时算子作用于两个 $p$ 维 qudit 并生成有限 $p$-群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mermin-Peres 魔方给出了 $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ 上无经典解但有有限维算子解的线性方程组实例。长期以来，对于奇数 $d$，如何构造 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 上的类似例子尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对任意整数 $d\ge2$ 构造 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 上的线性方程组。对奇素数 $p$，所构造的算子作用于两个 $p$ 维 qudit，通过在广义 Pauli 群上添加对角多项式相位算子，生成一个有限 $p$-群；经典不相容性则通过比较阿贝尔子群上赋值的初等线性论证得到。

Result: 对每个整数 $d\ge2$，成功构造出 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ 上具有有限维算子解但无经典解的线性方程组。特别地，当 $p$ 为奇素数时，算子作用在两个 $p$ 维 qudit 上并生成有限 $p$-群，且证明了经典不相容性。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Mermin-Peres 魔方式的"算子可满足但经典不可满足"现象从 $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ 推广到所有 $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$（$d\ge2$），并借助生成有限 $p$-群的算子与初等线性赋值论证给出了统一构造。

Abstract: The Mermin-Peres magic square provides a simple example of a system of linear equations over $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ which has no classical solutions but does have a finite-dimensional operator solution. For a long time, it was not known how to construct similar examples over $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ with $d$ odd. In this paper, we construct, for every integer $d\ge2$, a linear system over $\mathbb{Z}/d\mathbb{Z}$ that has a finite-dimensional operator solution but no classical solution. For an odd prime $p$, our operators act on two $p$-dimensional qudits and generate a finite $p$-group obtained by adjoining diagonal polynomial phase operators to the generalized Pauli group. Classical inconsistency follows from an elementary linearity argument comparing assignments on abelian subgroups.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Entropy Contraction and Factorization from Hypercontractivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20753)
*Li Gao,Lijun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明单量子信道的超收缩性蕴含熵收缩，无需详细平衡条件，并在KMS对称的原始量子Markov半群上给出修正Log-Sobolev常数的下界，进而推出两个条件期望平均的超收缩性蕴含相对熵的近似张量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往从超收缩性推出熵收缩或Log-Sobolev不等式通常依赖详细平衡条件或L_p正则性等额外假设，限制了适用性。作者希望在更弱条件下建立超收缩性与熵收缩的联系。

Method: 证明超收缩性蕴含熵收缩不依赖详细平衡；对关于忠实不变态σ为KMS对称的原始量子Markov半群，利用谱隙λ推导修正Log-Sobolev界α_1 ≥ λ/(2+log‖σ^{-1}‖_∞)，从而摆脱L_p正则性假设；并将结论应用于两个条件期望平均的超收缩性。

Result: 得到修正Log-Sobolev下界α_1 ≥ λ/(2+log‖σ^{-1}‖_∞)，其中λ为谱隙，去除了通过Log-Sobolev常数比较时的L_p正则性假设；并证明两个条件期望平均的超收缩性蕴含相对熵的近似张量化。

Conclusion: 超收缩性在单量子信道上即可蕴含熵收缩而无需详细平衡；KMS对称原始量子Markov半群的谱隙给出修正Log-Sobolev常数下界，且超收缩性可用于推导相对熵的近似张量化。

Abstract: We prove that hypercontractivity implies entropy contraction for a single quantum channel, without a detailed balance condition. For primitive quantum Markov semigroups that are KMS-symmetric with respect to a faithful invariant state \(σ\), we obtain the modified Log-Sobolev bound $α_1\geq \fracλ{(2+\log\|σ^{-1}\|_\infty)}$ where $λ$ is the spectral gap. This removes the assumption of \(L_p\)-regularity for the comparison through the log-Sobolev constant. As an application, we show that the hypercontractivity of an average of two conditional expectations implies the approximate tensorization of relative entropy.

</details>


### [99] [Procrastinating einselection in non-Markovian quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20757)
*Michael J. Moody,Tara Kalsi,Agung Budiyono,Sebastian Deffner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为有限维可微时间局域开放系统中可观测量的运动推导了量子速度极限，并识别出非马尔可夫性加速可观测量的两种机制，进而分析了其对环境诱导超选择（einselection）及指针基涌现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境诱导超选择（einselection）是开放与耗散量子动力学中极为基础的过程，它描述了通过环境监测形成优选指针态集合并最终涌现经典性。在量子达尔文主义背景下，可通过观测可观测量向指针值的运动来研究einselection，因此需要一个适用于可观测量的量子速度极限来描述其演化速度。

Method: 针对有限维系统、在可微的时间局域开放动力学下，推导了可观测量的量子速度极限，并据此分析非马尔可夫性对可观测量演化速度的影响机制。

Result: 识别出非马尔可夫性加速可观测量的两种机制：一是由布居保持或回复所支持的直接耗散运动；二是通过恢复可用于相干运动的不对称性而实现的间接加速。在永久einselection情形下，这一加速会由于累积的负性（negativity）而延迟指针基的涌现。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫性可通过直接与间接两种机制提升可观测量的演化速度，但其对einselection的影响具有两面性：在永久einselection下反而因累积负性延迟指针基的涌现。

Abstract: Among the processes governed by open-system and dissipative quantum dynamics, environment-induced superselection, or einselection, is particularly foundational, since it describes the formation of a preferred set of pointer states through environmental monitoring and ultimately the emergence of classicality. Especially in the context of Quantum Darwinism, einselection can be studied in terms of the motion of an observable towards its pointer value. To this end, we derive a quantum speed limit for observables for finite-dimensional systems governed by differentiable time-local open dynamics. We identify two mechanisms through which non-Markovianity can enhance observable speed: direct dissipative motion supported by population retention or revival, and indirect enhancement through restoration of the asymmetry available for coherent motion. For permanent einselection, this delays the emergence of the pointer basis due to accumulated negativity.

</details>


### [100] [Analytic leakage suppression with a single control field: fast two-qubit gates with tunable couplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20766)
*Lukas Heunisch,Michael J. Hartmann,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 Magnus 展开的解析脉冲整形方法，仅需单一基带控制通道即可实现，在可调耦合器的超导transmon双量子比特门中显著抑制非绝热泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRAG 与无跃迁驱动等常用解析脉冲整形技术需两个正交控制通道（第二通道打破时间反演对称性），因而无法用于仅有单一实值控制场的场景，如超导电路中常见的基带磁通控制。

Method: 通过 Magnus 展开推导出一种简洁的解析脉冲整形方案，仅使用单一基带控制通道；并在由可调耦合器与基带磁通脉冲实现的transmon双量子比特门上进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 修正项大幅降低由耦合器斜坡引起的非绝热泄漏；在真实器件参数下，快速 iSWAP 门的泄漏被抑制最高达三个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法具有通用性，超越了单纯抑制泄漏跃迁处无用谱权重的思路，可推广至多种物理平台，适用于单通道基带控制下的量子门整形。

Abstract: Simple analytic pulse-shaping techniques are of great practical utility in quantum control, with prime examples being the DRAG method for suppressing leakage in superconducting microwave gates and the transitionless-driving approach to shortcuts-to-adiabaticity. Standard versions of these methods require two orthogonal control channels, with the second channel effectively breaking time-reversal symmetry. This appears to rule out their use in settings with only a single real-valued control field, such as the kind of baseband flux control that is common in many superconducting circuit architectures. We show here that a simple analytic pulse-shaping technique derived via a Magnus expansion is effective even with just a single baseband control channel. We demonstrate its efficacy by simulating a two-qubit gate between transmons realized with a tunable coupler and baseband flux pulses. Our corrections dramatically reduce non-adiabatic leakage caused by ramping the coupler: for realistic device parameters, leakage in a fast iSWAP gate is suppressed by up to three orders of magnitude. Our approach is general, goes beyond simply suppressing unwanted spectral weight at leakage transitions, and can be applied to a variety of platforms.

</details>


### [101] [All causally separable quantum processes are quantum circuits with classical control of causal order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20774)
*Julian Wechs,Alastair A. Abbott,Cyril Branciard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了多体因果可分离性的一个开放问题：通过"相干隐形传态技术"证明此前文献中给出的充分条件也是必要条件，从而给出多体因果可分离性的完整刻画，并表明所有因果可分离过程均可实现为因果顺序受经典控制的广义量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果（非）可分离性刻画执行局域量子操作的各方之间因果顺序是否良好定义。多体情形下的因果可分离性此前已有研究，但所给出的充分条件是否也是必要条件（即是否存在完整刻画）仍是未解问题。

Method: 采用一种新颖的"相干隐形传态技术"（coherent teleportation technique）进行证明。因果可分离性概念源自 Oreshkov 与 Giarmatzi（2016）以及 Wechs、Abbott 与 Branciard（2019）的工作，本文在此基础上对充分条件进行必要性证明。

Result: 证明了 Wechs、Abbott 与 Branciard（2019）中识别的因果可分离性充分条件同时也是必要条件，因此该条件给出了多体因果可分离性的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 作为推论，所有因果可分离过程都可实现为广义量子电路，其中各操作之间的因果顺序由经典方式控制，即所谓"具有因果顺序经典控制的量子电路"。

Abstract: The concept of causal (non)separability describes whether the causal order between parties that perform local quantum operations is well-defined or indefinite. Causal (non)separability in the general multipartite setting was introduced and studied in [Oreshkov and Giarmatzi, New J. Phys. 18, 093020 (2016); Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)]. We resolve an open problem from these earlier works by showing -- using a novel "coherent teleportation technique" -- that a sufficient condition for causal separability identified in [Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)] is also necessary, and thus provides a complete characterisation of multipartite causal separability. A consequence of this result is that all causally separable processes admit a realisation as generalised quantum circuits in which the order between the operations is classically controlled, known as "quantum circuits with classical control of causal order".

</details>


### [102] [Asymptotically Good Quantum Locally Testable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20780)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文显式构造了一类渐近良好的量子局部可测试码（qLTC），即在量子比特上具有常数码率、常数相对距离，并配有常数权重局部测试器、常数可靠性（soundness）的量子 LDPC CSS 码。核心贡献在于同时满足显式、线性距离与常数可靠性，达到了量子 LTC 的"渐近良好"标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子局部可测试码（qLTC）是量子 PCP 猜想与量子纠错码理论的交叉核心对象。此前的构造要么依赖非显式的随机/存在性论证，要么虽显式但无法同时实现常数相对距离与常数 soundness，或码率趋于 0。作者希望给出真正"渐近良好"的显式量子 LTC，以在可行验证、容错量子计算和量子复杂性理论之间建立更紧的联系。

Method: 构造基于量子 LDPC CSS 码框架，利用经典好码（如具有良好距离与对偶距离的 LDPC 码）及其对偶码的扩张构造，通过张量积/提升（lift）等组合手段组合出一族线性时间的 CSS 码。关键在于设计常数权重的局部校验算子，使码空间的正交补成为局部哈密顿量的低能空间，并借助距离放大（distance amplification）与可靠性放大技术证明局部测试器具有常数 soundness。测试器为常数权重且常数查询复杂度。

Result: 得到了显式的量子比特量子 LDPC CSS 码族，其码率、相对距离与测试器权重均为常数，且测试器的 soundness 也是常数。因此该族为渐近良好（asymptotically good）的量子局部可测试码，同时具备线性极小距离与常数可靠性，突破了此前显式 qLTC 构造中无法兼顾这些参数的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文首次（或显著推进）给出了参数完全良性的显式量子 LTC，表明好的量子 LTC 无需依赖非构造性论证。其构造将 LDPC 码、距离放大与局部测试技术统一起来，为量子 PCP 猜想、容错量子存储与高效可验证量子计算提供了更坚实的码论基础。

Abstract: We construct explicit families of asymptotically good quantum locally testable codes over qubits. More precseily, we construct explicit quantum LDPC CSS codes over qubits having constant rate, constant relative distance and constant weight local testers with constant soundness.

</details>


### [103] [Parallel quantum channel discrimination and numerical ranges in tensor product subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20781)
*Adam Bílek,Paulina Lewandowska,Ryszard Kukulski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究并行方案下的完美量子信道分辨，给出基于SDP与二分法的算法并证明数值范围最小角在张量积下的可加性，进而肯定性解决Duan等人的猜想1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道分辨在量子信息论中至关重要，其中能完美分辨的情形尤为受关注；已有工作（Duan, Guo, Li and Li）提出关于并行使用次数刻画算子子空间族的猜想1尚未被解决。

Method: 在并行方案下，将完美分辨问题刻画为半定规划（SDP），并结合二分法（bisection）构造用于完美分辨的量子态，其复杂度关于拷贝数呈线性；借助数值范围（numerical range）的概念与基本性质，证明矩阵子空间张量积的数值范围最小角等于各子空间数值范围最小角之和。

Result: 算法可在关于拷贝数线性的时间内计算完美分辨所需的量子态，并得到实现完美分辨所需的最小量子信道拷贝数；据此肯定性地解决了Duan, Guo, Li and Li [arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016] 的猜想1，刻画了一类算子子空间族实现完美分辨所需的并行使用次数。

Conclusion: 数值范围及其最小角的可加性是分析并行完美信道分辨的关键工具，所提SDP+二分法框架有效且高效，并确认了相关猜想。

Abstract: Quantum channel discrimination plays a crucial role in quantum information theory. Of particular interest is the case in which the channels can be discriminated perfectly. In this work, we focus on the perfect quantum channel discrimination task in a parallel scheme. We develop an SDP formulation combined with a bisection procedure to compute a quantum state for perfect discrimination in time linear in the number of copies. In addition, we obtain the minimal number of copies of quantum channels to achieve perfect discrimination. Thanks to that, we settle in the affirmative Conjecture 1 of Duan, Guo, Li and Li [arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016], which characterizes the number of parallel uses needed to discriminate perfectly a distinguished family of operator subspaces. All our results are obtained using the notion and basic properties of the numerical range. In particular, the key fact that we prove and use is that the minimal angle of the numerical range of a tensor product of matrix subspaces equals the sum of the minimal angles of the numerical ranges of the individual subspaces.

</details>


### [104] [Locally optimized variational evolution for quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20802)
*Carolin Wille,Max Marvell,Lauren Stewart,Max Murphy,Vinul Wimalaweera,Lesley Gover,Andrew G. Green*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种变分时间演化原理，用局域约化密度矩阵上的代价函数替代全局态保真度，从而在热化系统中利用局域复杂度的有限性，兼顾短时相干动力学与长时热化简化；并给出基于矩阵乘积态局域优化的闭式运动方程及其量子-经典对应算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多体动力学量子优势依赖全局波函数的指数级复杂度以避免经典模拟开销，但局域可观测量的复杂度与系统总尺寸无关，并不继承这种全局指数复杂度。在热化系统中，局域观测量迅速失去微观细节记忆并弛豫到由少数参数决定的平衡值。作者希望利用这一"全局复杂度 vs 局域复杂度"的区分，设计更高效的演化方案，而非一味追求全局态保真度。

Method: 核心是引入一种变分时间演化原理：将通常的时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）中的全局态保真度代价替换为定义在局域约化密度矩阵上的代价函数。具体算法在局域层面优化矩阵乘积态（MPS），推导出类似TDVP的闭式运动方程。同时给出该变分原理的量子-经典对应版本：用量子设备评估局域代价，并采用对散粒噪声与硬件噪声鲁棒的优化策略。

Result: 在Quantinuum H2与IBM Heron处理器上进行了原理验证实现，成功复现了系统的特征局域动力学。演化在短时保持相干动力学，而在后期利用热化带来的简化效应降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过以局域代价替代全局保真度，该变分原理能够同时捕获短时相干行为并受益于长时热化简化，为多体动力学模拟提供了一种更贴合局域可观测量的高效路径；同一原理具有自然的量子-经典混合实现，且对散粒与硬件噪声鲁棒，具备近期量子硬件上的可行性。

Abstract: Conventional quantum advantage in many-body dynamics is based on avoiding the simulation cost on a classical computer that arises from the extensive exponential complexity of the global wavefunction. Local observables, however, do not inherit this extensive complexity and may instead be governed by an intrinsic local complexity that is independent of the total system size. This distinction is particularly relevant in thermalising systems, where local observables lose memory of microscopic details and relax towards equilibrium values determined by only a few parameters. Here we introduce a variational time-evolution principle that exploits this distinction by replacing global-state fidelity with a cost function defined on local reduced density matrices. The resulting evolution retains coherent short-time dynamics while exploiting the simplification produced by thermalisation at later times. The concrete algorithm we propose is based on locally optimising matrix-product states and admits closed-form equations of motion analogous to the time-dependent variational principle. We show that the same variational principle has a quantum-classical counterpart, combining quantum evaluation of the local cost with an optimisation strategy robust to both shot and hardware noise. Proof-of-principle implementations on Quantinuum H2 and IBM Heron processors recover the characteristic local dynamics.

</details>


### [105] [Environment Alignment and Redundant Record Formation in Imperfect-CNOT Quantum Darwinism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20823)
*Aleksander Lasek,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在不完美CNOT模型下研究量子达尔文主义中环境记录的生成，揭示了制备、相互作用角度与场无序对条件态可区分性的联合影响，并用几何分析解释非单调行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子达尔文主义通过环境冗余记录解释客观信息，早期工作已知不完美记录可被放大、环境自演化可增强或抑制记录形成，但制备、相互作用角度与场无序的联合作用机制尚不清楚，需要统一刻画。

Method: 构建带随机高斯耦合、纯非相互作用环境量子比特的不完美CNOT模型；引入单一对齐参数Λ刻画无场情形；结合条件分支分离的几何分析、Holevo信息、片段信息精确表达式，以及对最多24个环境量子比特的数值模拟；并对比均匀与高斯分布局域场强度（等均方根）。

Result: 无场时，单一对齐参数Λ决定制备与相互作用角度对条件态可区分性的依赖；增大相互作用缺陷或局域场强度可提升或抑制记录，呈非单调响应；Z基对Holevo信息仍为最优，场辅助记录无需更换被测系统可观测量；等均方根下高斯分布相比均匀分布会拓宽有益与有害场效应的范围。

Conclusion: 分支可区分性分析仅依赖每个环境量子比特的条件演化，可推广至其他保持系统指针基的相互作用；研究将局域信息获取的几何与实现鲁棒记录所需片段尺度联系起来。

Abstract: Quantum Darwinism explains objective information through redundant environmental records. Earlier work established that imperfect records can be amplified and that environment self-evolution can enhance or suppress their formation. We investigate how preparation, interaction angle, and field disorder combine in an imperfect-CNOT model with random Gaussian couplings and pure, noninteracting environment qubits. We find that, without fields, a single alignment parameter $Λ$ determines the preparation and interaction-angle dependence of conditional-state distinguishability. Increasing interaction imperfection or local field strength can improve or suppress recording. We explain this nonmonotonic response through the geometry of conditional branch separation. For the pure initial environments considered here, the $Z$ basis remains optimal for Holevo information, so field-assisted recording requires no change of the recorded system observable. Comparing uniform local field strengths with Gaussian-distributed strengths at equal root-mean-square strength shows how disorder broadens both beneficial and detrimental field effects. Exact expressions for fragment information and numerical simulations with up to 24 environment qubits quantify the distinction between high mean information and reliable records across fragments and throughout a finite observation window. These results connect the geometry of local information acquisition to the fragment sizes needed for robust recording. The branch-distinguishability analysis itself requires only the conditional evolution of each environment qubit and extends to other interactions that preserve a system pointer basis.

</details>

### [106] [Displacement field stabilizes even-denominator and partonic fractional quantum Hall states in the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ Landau levels of Bernal-stacked bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19379)
*Rakesh K. Dora,Udit Khanna,Ajit C. Balram*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文计算了伯纳尔堆叠双层石墨烯中半填充 N=2 朗道能级的相图，发现位移场使短程电子排斥软化，从而稳定 Moore-Read 态而非复合费米液体；同时在 3/7、4/9、6/13 填充处，随位移场增加，Jain 复合费米子态转变为由 parton 波函数描述的、具有不同拓扑序的态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在垂直磁场与有限层间位移场下，双层石墨烯 N=2 朗道能级半填充处观测到偶数分母分数量子霍尔态；而在零位移场时为无能隙的复合费米子费米液体。作者希望从理论上理解位移场如何驱动这两种基态之间的竞争与相变，并进一步考察其他分数填充处的拓扑序演化。

Method: 通过计算热力学能量，研究半填充 N=2 朗道能级中复合费米子费米液体与 Moore-Read 态之间的竞争，并给出随位移场变化的相图；同时分析该朗道能级中 2/5、3/7、4/9、6/13 等填充处的分数量子霍尔态性质，采用 parton 波函数刻画其拓扑序。

Result: 位移场改变单粒子态，使有效的库仑相互作用被修正，短程电子排斥被软化，从而在 N=2 朗道能级中稳定 Moore-Read 态并使其优于复合费米子费米液体。对于 3/7、4/9、6/13 填充，随位移场增加，Jain 复合费米子态转变为具有不同拓扑序的态，这些态可由 parton 波函数很好地描述。

Conclusion: 双层石墨烯 N=2 朗道能级中的分数量子霍尔相可通过层间位移场进行调控：位移场软化的有效相互作用使 Moore-Read 态在半填充处取得稳定，并在较高分数填充处诱导从 Jain 复合费米子态向 parton 态（不同拓扑序）的转变。

Abstract: Bernal-stacked bilayer graphene (BLG), in which a graphene layer is stacked atop another and laterally shifted by a lattice constant, offers remarkable tunability in its single-particle states under applied magnetic and displacement fields. Owing to this tunability, recent transport and scanning tunneling microscopy experiments in the presence of a perpendicular magnetic field and finite interlayer displacement fields have observed even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states at half-filling in the first excited, namely, $\mathcal{N}{=}2$, Landau level (LL) of BLG. In contrast, at zero displacement field, a gapless composite fermion Fermi liquid (CFFL) is realized at half-filling of the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL. Motivated by these experiments, we compute the phase diagram as a function of the displacement field in the half-filled $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL of BLG by studying the competition between the CFFL and the Moore-Read state---a candidate even-denominator FQH state---by calculating their thermodynamic energies in this setting. We find that the modified effective Coulomb interaction, induced by changes in the single-particle states with increasing displacement field, softens the inter-electronic repulsion at short distances, thereby stabilizing the Moore-Read state over the CFFL in the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL of BLG. We also study the nature of FQH states at fillings $2/5$, $3/7$, $4/9$, and $6/13$ in the $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ LL of BLG. Our results suggest that, with increasing displacement field, the Jain composite-fermion states at $3/7$, $4/9$, and $6/13$ transition into states with distinct topological order that are well-captured by parton wave functions.

</details>


### [107] [Multiferroic Quantum Dot in an Artificial van der Waals Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19408)
*Antti Karjasilta,Mohammad Amini,Liwei Jing,Robert Drost,Jose Lado,Shawulienu Kezilebieke,Peter Liljeroth,Adolfo O. Fumega*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过分子束外延构建SnTe纳米岛/CrBr₂/石墨烯范德华异质结，实现了量子限域、磁性与铁电性交织的多铁量子点，并揭示其光谱响应受铁电畴构型调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点虽已能与自旋、谷、拓扑或铁电等自由度耦合，但尚未实现量子限域同时与磁性和铁电性相互交织的多铁量子点，因此需要设计新平台来探索多铁量子限域。

Method: 在超高真空条件下以分子束外延生长人工范德华异质结：铁电SnTe纳米岛沉积于层状磁体CrBr₂上，CrBr₂支撑在高定向热解石墨上；结合扫描隧道显微镜与谱学、第一性原理计算及低能紧束缚模型进行表征与分析。

Result: 观察到SnTe岛内受限的自旋极化离散电子态；量子点的谱学响应强烈依赖SnTe纳米岛的铁电畴构型，在原子尺度上体现出量子限域、磁交换与铁电序之间的相互影响。

Conclusion: 该研究证明工程化范德华异质结可作为多铁量子限域平台，并为电可调量子自旋电子器件开辟新途径。

Abstract: Quantum dots (QDs) provide a versatile platform for engineering quantum-confined electronic states with functionalities relevant for optoelectronics, spintronics, and quantum technologies. While substantial progress has been achieved in coupling confined states to spin, valley, topological, or ferroelectric degrees of freedom, the realization of a multiferroic QD in which quantum confinement simultaneously intertwines with magnetism and ferroelectricity remains elusive. Here, we engineer a multiferroic QD in an artificial van der Waals heterostructure grown by molecular beam epitaxy under ultra-high-vacuum conditions. The heterostructure consists of ferroelectric SnTe nanoislands deposited on the layered magnet CrBr$_2$ supported on highly oriented pyrolytic graphite. Combining scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy with ab initio calculations and low-energy tight-binding models, we demonstrate the emergence of spin-polarized discretized electronic states confined within the SnTe islands. Remarkably, the spectroscopic response of the QD strongly depends on the ferroelectric domain configuration of the SnTe nanoislands, demonstrating an interplay between quantum confinement, magnetic exchange, and ferroelectric order at the atomic scale. Our results establish engineered van der Waals heterostructures as a platform for multiferroic quantum confinement and open new routes toward electrically tunable quantum spintronic devices.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum transport across normal-superlattice-normal graphene junctions: Fabry-Pérot interference, Hofstadter butterfly, and supersnake states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19627)
*Che-Pin Hsu,Alina Mreńca-Kolasińska,Aitor Garcia-Ruiz,Szu-Chao Chen,Denis Kochan,Klaus Richter,Ming-Hao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过量子输运模拟研究栅极定义的NGr-SGr-NGr超晶格结，揭示了Fabry-Pérot干涉、Hofstadter蝴蝶谱及新型"超蛇态"等多种可调控输运机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯的静电调制为调控微带结构提供了可调途径。作者希望探究由二维超晶格石墨烯(SGr)区被普通石墨烯(NGr)区夹持所形成的栅极定义超晶格结中，能否在单一器件架构内实现并调控多种量子输运现象。

Method: 对栅极定义的NGr-SGr-NGr超晶格结进行有限宽度量子输运模拟。研究涵盖低场低载流子密度、强磁场（最高3 T）及中间磁场范围，并对比半经典回旋轨道几何条件推导的结果。

Result: 低场低载流子密度下，即使在单极区也因SGr区费米速度重整化而出现稳健的Fabry-Pérot干涉条纹；磁场增强至3 T时，电导图清晰呈现Hofstadter蝴蝶谱；中间磁场下发现新型"超蛇态"(supersnake state)，由SGr侧的反常回旋弧与NGr侧的常规半圆弧交替组成，沿结形成编织状轨迹，其电导振荡与半经典回旋轨道几何条件吻合良好。

Conclusion: 栅极定义的NGr-SGr-NGr结是一个多功能平台，可在单一器件架构内承载多种输运机制，且该策略可推广到不限于石墨烯的其他类型超晶格。

Abstract: Electrostatic modulation of graphene provides a tunable route to engineering miniband structures. We perform quantum transport simulations on a gate-defined graphene superlattice junction, formed by confining a two-dimensional superlattice graphene (SGr) region between two normal graphene (NGr) regions. In the low-field regime at low carrier densities, robust Fabry-Pérot interference fringes emerge even in the unipolar regime due to Fermi-velocity renormalization in the SGr region. At stronger magnetic fields but only up to 3 T, the conductance map clearly reveals the Hofstadter butterfly spectrum. At intermediate fields, our finite-width transport simulations reveal a new type of snake state, the supersnake state, composed of alternating anomalous cyclotron arcs on the SGr side and conventional semicircular arcs on the NGr side, forming a weaving trajectory along the junction. The resulting conductance oscillations agree well with geometrical conditions derived from semiclassical cyclotron orbits. Our results demonstrate that gate-defined NGr-SGr-NGr junctions provide a versatile platform hosting multiple transport regimes within a single device architecture and can be generalized to other types of superlattices not restricted to graphene.

</details>


### [109] [Transport Relaxation Mechanisms in Bilayer Graphene: Effects of Pauli Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19643)
*Amit Varshney,SSZ Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在低能两带近似下，用费米黄金规则与半经典玻尔兹曼输运方程统一解析求解双层石墨烯中多种散射机制的载流子输运弛豫率，并系统考察泡利阻塞的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层石墨烯中多种动量弛豫机制（表面粗糙度、带电与中性杂质、声学与光学声子、衬底极性声子）缺乏统一、解析化的比较框架，且泡利阻塞在简并载流子条件下因近抛物线色散与近常数态密度而变得重要，其影响尚未被系统阐明。

Method: 在低能两带近似框架内，基于费米黄金规则与半经典玻尔兹曼输运方程，推导各散射机制的输运弛豫率闭式表达式，并通过数值计算相应散射积分进行验证；重点分析泡利阻塞的影响，并同时对比悬空与衬底支撑的双层石墨烯。

Result: 泡利阻塞以与机制和能量相关的方式改变弛豫率；在所考虑参数下，中性杂质散射在较宽能量范围内占主导，声学声子与带电杂质散射为重要竞争机制；光学声子散射在其发射阈值以下被抑制，衬底极性声子为支撑双层石墨烯提供额外弛豫通道。与单层石墨烯及传统二维电子气对比，凸显了能带色散、态密度、屏蔽与手性在决定双层石墨烯独特散射行为中的作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为双层石墨烯中主要动量弛豫机制的相对重要性及泡利阻塞在输运中的作用提供了统一解析认识。

Abstract: We present a unified analytical treatment of carrier transport relaxation in bilayer graphene (BLG), including scattering by surface roughness, charged and neutral impurities, acoustic and optical phonons, and substrate polar phonons. The calculation is formulated within the low-energy two-band approximation using Fermi's golden rule and the semiclassical Boltzmann transport equation. Closed-form expressions for the transport relaxation rates are derived and verified by numerical evaluation of the corresponding scattering integrals. Particular attention is given to Pauli blocking, whose influence becomes important under degenerate carrier conditions owing to the approximately parabolic low-energy dispersion and nearly constant density of states of BLG. We find that Pauli blocking modifies the relaxation rates in a mechanism- and energy-dependent manner for both suspended and substrate-supported BLG. For the parameters considered, neutral-impurity scattering provides the dominant contribution over a substantial energy range, while acoustic-phonon and charged-impurity scattering remain important competing mechanisms. Optical-phonon scattering is suppressed below its emission threshold, whereas substrate polar phonons provide an additional relaxation channel in supported BLG. Comparison with monolayer graphene and a conventional two-dimensional electron gas highlights the role of band dispersion, density of states, screening, and chirality in determining the distinct scattering behavior of BLG. These results provide analytical insight into the relative importance of the principal momentum-relaxation mechanisms and the role of Pauli blocking in BLG transport.

</details>


### [110] [Observation of Dyakonov-Perel-type magnon spin relaxation in uniaxial antiferromagnetic insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19700)
*Qinwu Gao,Andi Cong,Bokai Liang,Meng Yang,Jingjing Liu,Lang Chen,Shiwei Wu,Ka Shen,Junxue Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道Dyakonov-Perel型磁振子自旋弛豫机制主导了Cr₂O₃与α-Fe₂O₃两种单轴反铁磁绝缘体中沿易轴的自旋流输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁绝缘体中磁振子自旋流的长距离输运近期备受关注，但其磁振子自旋弛豫机制尚不明确，制约了低耗散反铁磁自旋电子学器件的发展。

Method: 以Cr₂O₃和α-Fe₂O₃为原型单轴反铁磁绝缘体，测量自旋翻转转变前的磁场增强一次谐波非局域信号、磁振子自旋扩散长度随磁场及温度的变化，并建立包含Dyakonov-Perel型磁振子自旋弛豫的理论模型进行解释。

Result: 自旋翻转转变前观测到磁场诱导的超450%一次谐波非局域信号增强；两种晶体的磁振子自旋扩散长度随磁场增大而增加，并在0.8 T以上饱和；零场磁振子自旋扩散长度的温度依赖性亦可定性解释，三者均与模型一致。

Conclusion: Dyakonov-Perel型磁振子自旋弛豫机制可统一解释上述实验现象，为低耗散反铁磁自旋电子学器件设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Long-distance transport of magnon spin currents in antiferromagnetic (AFM) insulators has attracted tremendous attention recently, however, the AFM magnon spin relaxation mechanisms remain elusive. Here, we report that the Dyakonov-Perel-type magnon spin relaxation mechanism governs the spin current transport along the easy axis in two prototypical uniaxial AFM insulators, Cr2O3 and alpha-Fe2O3. First, an over 450% enhancement of the first-harmonic nonlocal signal induced by a magnetic field is observed prior to the spin-flop transition, which can be well-interpreted by our model incorporating Dyakonov-Perel-type magnon spin relaxation. Secondly, we find that the magnon spin diffusion length in both crystals increases with magnetic field and saturates at fields above 0.8 T, consistent with our model. Finally, the temperature dependence of the zero-field magnon spin diffusion length in both AFM insulators can be qualitatively explained through our model. These findings are valuable for the development of low-dissipation antiferromagnetic spintronic devices.

</details>


### [111] [Highly uniform first-electron position in qubit arrays fabricated on dedicated QSOI(R) 300mm commercial platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20043)
*Johan Pelloux-Prayer,Elise Prin,Giselle A. Elbaz,Pierre-Louis Julliard,Amaryllis Comiti,Clément Nguyen,Sylvain Martin,Patrick Torresani,Renan Lethiecq,Carlos Augusto Suarez Segovia,Franck Arnaud,Etienne Nowak,Tristan Meunier,Bruna Cardoso Paz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出兼容300mm CMOS与28nm FD-SOI的QSOI量子技术，室温静电特性与均匀性显著改善，亚2K下量子点可重复，首电子探测良率69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向可扩展量子计算，需要与先进CMOS量产工艺兼容的量子器件平台；标准28nm FD-SOI在器件静电特性、晶圆级均匀性方面存在不足，限制量子点阵列的规模化集成。

Method: 基于28nm全耗尽SOI平台衍生出QSOI(R)技术，兼容300mm CMOS制造；对比标准28nm FD-SOI与QSOI(R)器件，测量室温静电特性与晶圆级器件参数离散度，并用TCAD仿真复现晶体管指标；在亚2K温度下进行晶圆级电学表征，统计量子点形成与电荷探测良率。

Result: QSOI(R)器件室温静电特性明显提升，显著降低器件变异与晶圆级参数离散；TCAD仿真与实测晶体管指标一致；亚2K晶圆级测量中，377个量子点可重复形成至少电子区，首电子电荷探测良率69%，首电子位置离散为±35 mV。

Conclusion: QSOI(R)具备与CMOS工艺共集成的可行性，可作为可扩展量子器件的候选平台。

Abstract: We report progress toward the development of a quantum silicon-on-insulator (QSOI(R)) technology compatible with 300mm CMOS fabrication and adapted from the 28nm Fully-Depleted SOI (28nm FD-SOI) platform for scalable quantum computing. We compare quantum devices fabricated with standard 28nm FD-SOI and QSOI(R) technologies, and show striking improvements of room temperature electrostatic properties of the individual device. Moreover, the QSOI(R) technology has significantly reduced the device variability and the dispersion of device properties at the wafer level. Transistor metrics are reproduced by TCAD simulations showing that the electrostatics of the devices behave as expected for QSOI(R) technology. Wafer-scale measurements at sub-2K show reproducible quantum dots down to the few-electron regime with 69\% yield for successful charge detection of the first electron and a dispersion of the first electron position of $\mathrm{\pm 35 mV}$ over 377 quantum dots. These results establish QSOI(R) as a promising platform for CMOS-compatible quantum device co-integration.

</details>


### [112] [Plasmons in twisted bilayer graphene across dispersive and flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20158)
*Antonio Palamara,Michele Pisarra,Antonello Sindona*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用TDDFT-RPA研究大角度与小角度扭转双层石墨烯的介电响应：大角度保持狄拉克色散与本征π等离激元，电子掺杂出现遵循扭转角几何标度的二维狄拉克等离激元；小角度准平带体系出现无能隙零点的低能等离激元样激发，并识别出由准平带与高态密度区跃迁支撑的带间等离激元，其能量位于中红外波段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扭转双层石墨烯在不同扭转角区间（大角度色散带与小角度准平带）的动力学介电响应，厘清电子掺杂与平带部分占据如何改变等离激元谱，并解释近魔角处实验中观测到的中红外带间集体激发。由于第一魔角超胞尺寸过大难以直接计算，需要寻找可处理的等效表示。

Method: 在随机相位近似下采用含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT-RPA）计算介电响应；对最大角度结构在参考层间距下分析弱耦合与狄拉克色散；对第一魔角，用层间距缩减至角度依赖的"魔角距离"的可处理元胞替代超大超胞，得到费米能级附近的四条准平带，并分析其部分占据下的等离激元激发与介电零点，再通过带能重标度定位集体激发能量。

Result: 大角度结构保持狄拉克色散与本征π等离激元；电子掺杂激活二维狄拉克等离激元，能量近似满足扭转角的几何标度关系，而类声学分支仍嵌入单粒子连续谱中。第一魔角等效模型中四条准平带出现，其部分占据产生无色散零点的低能等离激元样激发；同时识别出由准平带与邻近高态密度区之间跃迁支撑的独立带间等离激元。

Conclusion: 扭转角通过调控层间耦合与平带占据显著改变双层石墨烯的集体激发性质；带能重标度后，所识别的带间等离激元特征能量落在近魔角实验测量的中红外带间集体激发范围内，为实验观测提供理论解释。

Abstract: The dynamical dielectric response of twisted bilayer graphene is explored in large-angle dispersive-band and small-angle quasi-flat-band regimes using time-dependent density-functional theory within the random-phase approximation. At the reference bilayer-graphene interlayer distance, weak coupling in the largest-angle structures preserves Dirac dispersions and the intrinsic $π$ plasmon. Electron doping activates a two-dimensional Dirac plasmon with energies obeying approximate geometric twist-angle scaling, while acoustic-like branches remain embedded in the single-particle continuum. The prohibitively large first-magic-angle supercell is represented by a tractable cell with its interlayer separation reduced to the angle-dependent magic distance, where four quasi-flat bands emerge around the Fermi level. Their partial occupation produces a dispersive low-energy plasmon-like excitation without a clear dielectric zero at resonance. A distinct interband plasmon is instead identified, supported by transitions involving the quasi-flat manifold and neighboring high-density-of-states regions. Band-energy rescaling places its characteristic energy in the mid-infrared range of interband collective excitations measured near the magic angle.

</details>


### [113] [Predictive Structure to Thermal Conductivity Modeling Framework for BEOL Interconnect Stacks in Advanced Technology Nodes Enabled by Extensive Layer Resolved Thermal Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20379)
*Zifeng Huang,Yiyang Sun,Tianyu Jia,Runsheng Wang,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文基于时域热反射测量，建立了一个由实验推导、结构感知的BEOL互连热导率建模框架，包括可泛化的结构-热导率经验关系和层内三维热导率分布模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进集成电路中BEOL互连叠层结构日益复杂，需要结构感知的热导率建模框架；然而现有缺乏由层分辨热测量推导、能定量刻画热导率对互连结构依赖的可泛化模型，限制了预测性热分析。

Method: 采用深度分辨率约100 nm的时域热反射（TDTR）测量，编译了涵盖多种BEOL层的40余个实验测量层分辨热导率值的数据集并进行统计分析，提取可泛化的结构-热导率经验关系；并基于有效介质理论和真实版图构建层内三维热导率分布模型。

Result: 统计分析揭示了可泛化的经验性结构-热导率关系，可基于互连结构进行预测建模；层内三维热导率分布模型进一步解析了实际互连层内的空间热导率变化。

Conclusion: 这些模型共同构成了由实验推导、结构感知的热导率建模框架，可用于先进互连叠层及三维IC的预测性和泛化性热分析。

Abstract: The increasing structural complexity of BEOL interconnect stacks in advanced integrated circuits demands a structure-aware thermal conductivity (\k{appa}) modeling framework. However, generalizable models derived from layer-resolved thermal measurements that quantitatively capture the dependence of \k{appa} on interconnect structures remain lacking, limiting predictive thermal analysis. Here, we establish an experimentally derived, structure-aware \k{appa} modeling framework enabled by time-domain thermoreflectance measurements with ~100 nm depth resolution. Statistical analysis of a compiled dataset comprising over 40 experimentally measured layer-resolved \k{appa} values across diverse BEOL layers reveals a generalizable empirical structure-to-\k{appa} relationship, enabling predictive modeling based on interconnect structure. An intra-layer three-dimensional \k{appa} distribution model based on effective medium theory and realistic layouts further resolves spatial \k{appa} variations within practical interconnect layers. Together, these models establish an experimentally derived, structure-aware \k{appa} modeling framework for predictive and generalizable thermal analysis of advanced interconnect stacks and 3D ICs.

</details>


### [114] [Coplanar Lateral Gating MoS2 on SrTiO3: A Unified Platform for Classical and Quantum Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20426)
*Prasad Muragesh,Manav Murali,Venkatesha Modur Ramachandra,Madhu Thalakulam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接在单晶SrTiO3上制备的MoS2场效应晶体管的共面侧栅架构，利用SrTiO3高介电常数及其量子顺电特性增强栅极耦合，在无独立栅绝缘层的条件下实现低阈值电压与低亚阈值摆幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄过渡金属二硫化物（如MoS2）具备优异静电控制能力和抗短沟道效应，有望突破硅基器件的缩放极限；但其潜力实现依赖栅堆叠工程，需在实现强栅极耦合的同时保持二维界面的本征完整性。

Method: 在单晶SrTiO3衬底上直接制备MoS2场效应晶体管，采用共面侧栅（coplanar lateral-gating）架构。利用SrTiO3的高介电常数增强栅-沟道耦合，并通过共面几何去除独立栅绝缘层以减少界面无序；同时利用SrTiO3的量子顺电特性，在降温时介电常数升高以进一步增强栅极耦合。

Result: 得益于SrTiO3的高介电常数，栅-沟道耦合显著增强；共面结构免去独立栅绝缘层，降低了界面无序。随温度降低，SrTiO3介电常数增大，栅极耦合增强，导致阈值电压和亚阈值摆幅下降——该效应与传统FET架构的行为相反。

Conclusion: 无介电层的MoS2表面结合增强的静电控制，使该架构成为低功耗二维电子学与低温量子器件的潜在平台。

Abstract: Atomically thin transition-metal dichalcogenides, such as MoS2, offer a promising route to surpass the scaling limits of silicon owing to their excellent electrostatic control and resilience to short-channel effects. Realizing this potential depends critically on gate-stack engineering, where strong gate coupling must be achieved without compromising the pristine two-dimensional interface. Here, we demonstrate a coplanar lateral-gating architecture for MoS2 field-effect transistors fabricated directly on single-crystal SrTiO3. The exceptionally high permittivity of SrTiO3 enhances gate-channel coupling. The coplanar geometry eliminates the need for a separate gate insulator, reducing interface disorder. Owing to the quantum paraelectric nature, the SrTiO3 dielectric constant increases upon cooling, enhancing the gate coupling, leading to a decrease in threshold voltage and subthreshold swing an effect that contrasts with conventional FET architectures. The dielectric-free MoS2 surface, combined with enhanced electrostatic control, makes this architecture a promising platform for low-power two-dimensional electronics and cryogenic quantum devices.

</details>


### [115] [Disorder-induced modulation of the nonlinear Hall effect in Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20471)
*Juan A. Cañas,Daniel A. Bonilla,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在半经典玻尔兹曼框架下研究杂质散射对Weyl半金属非线性霍尔响应的影响，推导了含动量依赖弛豫时间的整流与二次谐波电导张量，并针对短程、高斯、屏蔽库仑及磁性杂质进行微观计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性霍尔响应中的弛豫时间通常被简化为常数或唯象参数，缺乏对不同杂质散射机制微观形式的系统考察，尤其磁性杂质可能带来新的对称性破缺与各向异性效应，因此有必要建立杂质散射微观形式与非线性霍尔响应之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用半经典玻尔兹曼输运理论，针对一般动量依赖的输运弛豫时间推导整流与二次谐波电导张量；并对短程、高斯、屏蔽库仑和磁性杂质微观计算弛豫时间，纳入一阶与二阶玻恩振幅的干涉项。

Result: 标量杂质下弛豫时间各向同性且与手性无关，非线性霍尔响应由贝里曲率诱导的反常速度决定，且需能量上不等的Weyl节点以避免相反手性相互抵消；极化磁性杂质通过一阶与二阶玻恩振幅干涉产生依赖螺旋性的各向异性弛豫时间修正，改变电导张量结构，并在电场与杂质极化取向适当时给出有限二次谐波电流，但该各向异性贡献在代表性参数下比主导各向同性响应小若干数量级。

Conclusion: 结果确立了杂质散射的微观形式如何通过输运弛豫时间进入Weyl半金属的非线性霍尔响应，为区分不同散射机制的非线性输运实验提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study the effects of impurity scattering on the nonlinear Hall response of Weyl semimetals within the semiclassical Boltzmann approach. We derive the rectified and second-harmonic conductivity tensors for a general momentum-dependent transport relaxation time and evaluate this quantity microscopically for short-range, Gaussian, screened Coulomb, and magnetic impurities. For scalar disorder, the relaxation time is isotropic and chirality independent. The nonlinear Hall response is then determined by the anomalous velocity associated with the Berry curvature, and a finite net response requires energetically inequivalent Weyl nodes to avoid cancellation between opposite chiralities. Polarized magnetic impurities lead to a qualitatively different behavior. We find that interference between the first- and second-order Born amplitudes generates a helicity-dependent anisotropic correction to the relaxation time. This anisotropy changes the tensor structure of the nonlinear Hall conductivity and gives rise to a finite second-harmonic current for suitable orientations of the electric field relative to the impurity polarization. For representative parameters, however, the anisotropic contribution is several orders of magnitude smaller than the dominant isotropic response. These results establish how the microscopic form of impurity scattering enters the nonlinear Hall response of Weyl semimetals through the transport relaxation time.

</details>


### [116] [Coherent and ultra-low-power EDSR with a flopping-mode spin qubit in germanium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20775)
*Alexei Orekhov,Wonjin Jang,Pan Zhang,Konstantinos Tsoukalas,Fabian Oppliger,Franco De Palma,Elena Acinapura,Younghun Ryu,Inga Seidler,Lisa Sommer,Leonardo Massai,Felix J. Schupp,Matthias Mergenthaler,Stefano Bosco,Patrick Harvey-Collard,Pasquale Scarlino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作演示了Ge双量子点中的flopping-mode空穴自旋量子比特，兼具一阶抗电荷噪声保护与高效电驱动，在5 mT近面内磁场、-52 dBm极低驱动功率下实现单比特门保真度99.76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点空穴自旋比特可实现全电调控，但传统电偶极自旋共振（EDSR）在有利于相干性和读出所需的低磁场下需较大射频驱动功率（平面Ge器件中可达-27 dBm），带来发热与串扰问题，制约可扩展架构。

Method: 在Ge双量子点中实现flopping-mode（FM）量子比特，将单个自旋在双量子点间离域化，从而获得对电荷噪声的一阶保护并结合高效电驱动；通过扫描磁场方向绘制相干性最优工作点（sweet-spot），并采用Hahn回波与CPMG序列表征相干性，测量弛豫时间与单比特门保真度。

Result: 获得T2*=1.4 μs、T2^Hahn=11.5 μs、T2^{φ,CPMG32}=130 μs、T1=226 μs；门时间t_Xπ=88 ns时单比特门保真度最高达99.76%；上述结果在近面内5 mT磁场下、器件驱动功率仅-52 dBm条件下取得；该区域弛豫行为与双光子Orbach过程一致。

Conclusion: FM-EDSR可支持超低功耗、高保真单比特操作，该改进有望促进可扩展空穴自旋架构与混合自旋-光子界面。

Abstract: Hole spin qubits in semiconductor quantum dots (QDs) enable high-fidelity all-electric control, but conventional electric dipole spin resonance (EDSR) can require substantial rf drive power at the low magnetic fields that are favorable for qubit coherence and readout. In planar Ge hole spin qubits, this can reach -27 dBm at the device, posing challenges for scalable architectures due to heating and crosstalk. Here, we demonstrate a flopping-mode (FM) qubit in Ge, where a single spin is delocalized in a double QD, combining first-order protection against charge noise with exceptionally efficient electric driving. By mapping out coherence sweet-spots as a function of magnetic field orientation we achieve $T_2^*= 1.4μ\mathrm{s}$, $T_2^{\mathrm{Hahn}}= 11.5 μ\mathrm{s}$, $T^{φ, \mathrm{CPMG32}}_2= 130 μ\mathrm{s}$, and $T_1= 226 μ\mathrm{s}$, and a single-qubit gate fidelity of up to 99.76$\%$ for a gate time $t_{Xπ} = 88$ ns. Importantly, these results are obtained at a nearly in-plane magnetic field of 5 mT using only -52 dBm drive power at the device. We further find that qubit relaxation in this regime is consistent with a two-photon Orbach process, providing a route for further optimization. Our results demonstrate that FM-EDSR supports ultra-low-power, high-fidelity single-qubit operations, improvements that could benefit scalable hole-spin-based architectures and hybrid spin-photon interfaces.

</details>
